Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The microwave operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 299. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 691 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by microwave generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 299, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 691. QED.
